{"forms":[{"form":"p-adic norms","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A p-adic absolute value, for a given prime number p, the function, denoted |..|ₚ and defined on the rational numbers, such that |0|ₚ = 0 and, for x≠0, |x|ₚ = p^(-ordₚ(x)), where ordₚ(x) is the p-adic ordinal of x; the same function, extended to the p-adic numbers ℚₚ (the completion of the rational numbers with respect to the p-adic ultrametric defined by said absolute value); the same function, further extended to some extension of ℚₚ (for example, its algebraic closure)."],"topics":["mathematics","number-theory","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A norm on a vector space which is defined over a field equipped with a discrete valuation (a generalisation of p-adic absolute value)."],"topics":["algebra","mathematics","sciences"]}],"synonyms":[{"word":"p-adic absolute value"}],"upstream_line":4021050,"upstream_offset":9901036323,"upstream_record_sha256":"230e681832b73b0198b5fa090726fdcff06886a9bbf028232a7ab884ce9d0e8f","word":"p-adic norm"}

{"forms":[{"form":"stases","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A slackening or arrest of the blood current, due not to a lessening of the heart’s beat, but to some abnormal resistance of the capillary walls."],"tags":["uncountable","usually"],"topics":["medicine","pathology","sciences"]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"movement"},{"word":"flux"}],"examples":[{"text":"His company was sized for growth, not stasis.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Inactivity; a freezing, or state of motionlessness."],"synonyms":[{"word":"stability"},{"word":"staticity"}],"tags":["figuratively","uncountable","usually"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I was in stasis for forty years before I woke up orbiting this poxy planet!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A technology allowing something to be artificially frozen in time, so that it does not age or change."],"tags":["uncountable","usually"],"topics":["literature","media","publishing","science-fiction"]},{"glosses":["One of the sections of a cathisma or portion of the psalter."],"tags":["uncountable","usually"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈsteɪsɪs/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈstæ-/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈsteɪsɪs/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":233682,"upstream_offset":1144504673,"upstream_record_sha256":"a8b8b4cae33a20bab7d69e3eb68231da6041e213eeb1f900c71c9fab787e1df1","word":"stasis"}

{"forms":[{"form":"steps back","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"stepping back","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"stepped back","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"stepped back","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["Used other than figuratively or idiomatically: see step, back."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Perhaps we should step back for a second and think about solving this problem a different way.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To stop what one is doing and evaluate the current situation."],"tags":["idiomatic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"As a therapist sometimes you have to step back from your clients' lives.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To prevent oneself from becoming emotionally involved in a certain situation."],"tags":["idiomatic"]},{"glosses":["to depart driving the train following the train they arrived into the station driving, so as to decrease service turnaround time."],"raw_tags":["of a driver at a terminal station"],"topics":["rail-transport","railways","transport"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He's stepping back from the role to let you start in it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To retreat from one's duties in a job; to reduce one's duties, often as a prelude to leaving a position; to take a back seat."],"tags":["idiomatic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"You need to step back from the threat you made.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To quietly abandon a belief."],"tags":["idiomatic"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/stɛp ˈbæk/","tags":["Canada","General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/step ˈbæk/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"/step ˈbɛk/","tags":["New-Zealand"]}],"upstream_line":2681931,"upstream_offset":7946301347,"upstream_record_sha256":"d301f50ae6480d1b6575595ed9f277ff7a07c88d6445a9e5582c190cf2fb245f","word":"step back"}
{"forms":[{"form":"steps back","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"antonyms":[{"word":"leg up"}],"examples":[{"text":"Switching to more frequent meetings was a step back for that team, which had too many meetings already.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A setback, downgrade, or deterioration."],"tags":["idiomatic"]},{"glosses":["A reversion to a former state or situation."],"synonyms":[{"word":"deconversion"},{"word":"regression"},{"word":"restitution"},{"word":"deconversion"},{"word":"regression"},{"word":"restitution"},{"word":"restoration"},{"word":"reversion"},{"tags":["idiomatic"],"word":"step back"}],"tags":["idiomatic"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/stɛp ˈbæk/","tags":["Canada","General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/step ˈbæk/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"/step ˈbɛk/","tags":["New-Zealand"]}],"upstream_line":2681932,"upstream_offset":7946306764,"upstream_record_sha256":"daca03eb33584fadc3489c8efcf3839a358ca35bc4258362873bee41690eaaaf","word":"step back"}
